+SQuAD v2.0 dev (arXiv:1806.03822) contains
     35 articles,
     1204 paragraphs and
     11873 questions, of which
     5928 are answerable. Only the answerable rows are used;
     impossible rows are a diﬀerent kind of negative and are out of scope for this study. Articles
+
 are split
     whole, never at paragraph level, into a dev half and a held-out half. From each half we
+
 sample four
     hundred answerable questions and present each question with ﬁve passages, one per rung:
     The ladder varies the negative in one respect at a time — unrelated text (R1), topically
+
 anchored text
     (R2), adjacent text from the same document (R3), and text that is the gold passage except
+
 for one span
     (R4) — but not in only one respect: the rungs also diﬀer in length and in provenance, which
+
 is why ğ4.4
     and ğ4.6 use controls rather than assuming the rungs are otherwise matched. This yields
+
 3752 items
     (1879 held out); R2 is short at
     273 dev items because most questions have no content word that
     occurs in a paragraph other than their own. Following the pre-registration the rung is
+
 reported at its
     true size rather than topped up, and ğ4.7 checks that the questions which kept an R2 item

     diﬀerent from those which did not.
